floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, \n                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path, \n                   const size_t numNodes) {\n    // Classic Floyd-Warshall algorithm\n    // For each intermediate node k\n    for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {\n        // For each source node i\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {\n            // For each destination node j\n            for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {\n                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];\n                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];\n                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];\n                \n                const unsigned int newDist = distIK + distKJ;\n                \n                if (newDist < distIJ) {\n                    dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = newDist;\n                    path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = k;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {\n    // Basic sanity checks\n    \n    // 1. Diagonal should be zero\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {\n        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\\n\", i, i);\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]\n    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++j) {\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {\n                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];\n                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];\n                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];\n                \n                // Check for overflow before addition\n                if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF) {\n                    if (distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {\n                        printf(\"Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]\\n\", \n                               i, j, k);\n                        return false;\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of nodes in the graph (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t numNodes = 512;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numNodes = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Number of nodes: %zu\\n\", numNodes);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    \n    // Allocate matrices\n    std::vector<unsigned int> dist(numNodes * numNodes);\n    std::vector<unsigned int> path(numNodes * numNodes);\n    \n    // Initialize\n    printf(\"Initializing graph...\\n\");\n    initializeDistanceMatrix(dist, numNodes, 1, MAX_DISTANCE);\n    initializePathMatrix(path, numNodes);\n    \n    // Run Floyd-Warshall\n    printf(\"Computing shortest paths...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    \n    floydWarshall(dist, path, numNodes);\n    \n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n    \n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n    \n    // Calculate operations per second\n    // Floyd-Warshall has O(n³) complexity\n    double ops = (double)numNodes * numNodes * numNodes;\n    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f GOPS\\n\", gflops);\n    \n    // Print results for external validation (integer hash-based)\n    if (printResults) {\n        print_results_int(dist, \"DistanceMatrix\");\n    }\n    \n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n        bool valid = validateResult(dist, numNodes);\n        \n        if (valid) {\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return 0;\n}\ncmake_minimum_required(VERSION Every Floyd-Warshall step k needs the\n// full row k, which the owning rank broadcasts to everyone.\n\n// Block row distribution: first (n % size) ranks get one extra row.\ninline size_t rowBegin(const size_t numNodes, const int rank, const int size) noexcept {\n    const size_t base = numNodes / static_cast<size_t>(size);\n    const size_t rem = numNodes % static_cast<size_t>(size);\n    const size_t r = static_cast<size_t>(rank);\n    return r * base + std::min(r, rem);\n}\n\ninline int rowOwner(const size_t row, const size_t numNodes, const int size) noexcept {\n    const size_t base = numNodes / static_cast<size_t>(size);\n    const size_t rem = numNodes % static_cast<size_t>(size);\n    if (base == 0) {\n        return static_cast<int>(row);\n    }\n    if (row < rem * (base + 1)) {\n        return static_cast<int>(row / (base + 1));\n    }\n    return static_cast<int>(rem + (row - rem * (base + 1)) / base);\n}\n\n// Generates exactly the same sequence as the serial code, but keeps only the rows\n// owned by this rank (the RNG is inherently sequential, so the cheap O(n^2) stream\n// is replayed everywhere while only O(n^2 / p) values are stored).\nvoid initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes,\n                              const size_t firstRow, const size_t localRows,\n                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {\n    unsigned int seed = 42;\n    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;\n\n    const size_t begin = firstRow * numNodes;\n    const size_t end = begin + localRows * numNodes;\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; For step k the owner of row k broadcasts it; all\n    // ranks then relax their own rows against it. The pivot row for step k+1 is\n    // produced (and broadcast non-blockingly) before the bulk of step k's work, so\n    // the broadcast overlaps with computation.\n    if (numNodes == 0) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    std::vector<unsigned int> kRowBuf(2 * numNodes);\n    unsigned int* buf[2] = {kRowBuf.data(), kRowBuf.data() + numNodes};\n    MPI_Request req[2] = {MPI_REQUEST_NULL, MPI_REQUEST_NULL};\n\n    const size_t lastRow = firstRow + localRows;\n    const int intN = static_cast<int>(numNodes);\n\n    // Prime the pipeline with row 0.\n    {\n        const int owner = rowOwner(0, numNodes, size);\n        if (0 >= firstRow && 0 < lastRow) {\n            std::copy_n(dist.data(), numNodes, buf[0]);\n        }\n        MPI_Ibcast(buf[0], intN, MPI_UNSIGNED, owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &req[0]);\n    }\n\n    for (size_t k = 0; (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help          }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"Number of nodes: %zu\\n\", numNodes);\n        printf(\"MPI ranks: %d\\n\", size);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    }\n\n    // More ranks than rows would leave ranks idle in a way the row broadcast cannot\n    // express; clamp the number of participating ranks in that case.\n    if (static_cast<size_t>(size) > numNodes && numNodes > 0) {\n        size = static_cast<int>(numNodes);\n    }\n    MPI_Comm comm = MPI_COMM_WORLD;\n    if (rank >= size) {\n        // Idle ranks: nothing to do but participate in the final barrier.\n        MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n        MPI_Finalize();\n        return 0;\n    }\n\n    const size_t firstRow = rowBegin(numNodes, rank, size);\n    const size_t localRows = numNodes);\n\n    // Initialize\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Initializing graph...\\n\");\n    }\n    initializeDistanceMatrix(dist, numNodes, firstRow, localRows, 1, MAX_DISTANCE);\n The pivot row for step k+1 is\n    // produced (and broadcast non-blockingly) before the bulk of step k's work, so\n    // the broadcast overlaps with computation.\n    if (numNodes == 0) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    std::vector<unsigned int> kRowBuf(2 * numNodes);\n    unsigned int* buf[2] = {kRowBuf.data(), kRowBuf.data() + numNodes};\n    MPI_Request req[2] = {MPI_REQUEST_NULL, MPI_REQUEST_NULL};\n\n    const size_t lastRow = firstRow + localRows;\n    const int intN = static_cast<int>(numNodes);\n\n    // Prime the pipeline with row 0.\n    {\n        const int owner = rowOwner(0, numNodes, size);\n        if (0 >= firstRow && 0 < lastRow) {\n            std::copy_n(dist.data(), numNodes, buf[0]);\n        }\n        MPI_Ibcast(buf[0], intN, MPI_UNSIGNED, owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &req[0]);\n    }\n\n    for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {\n        const int cur = static_cast<int>(k & 1);\n        const int nxt = cur ^ 1;\n        MPI_Wait(&req[cur], MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);\n        const unsigned int* __restrict kRow = buf[cur];\n        const unsigned int ku = static_cast<unsigned int>(k);\n\n        // Produce and publish the next pivot row first, so its broadcast can\n        // progress while the remaining rows are relaxed.\n        const size_t next = k + 1;\n        bool ownsNext = false;\n        if (next < numNodes) {\n            ownsNext = (next >= firstRow && next < lastRow);\n            if (ownsNext) {\n                const size_t local = next - firstRow;\n                unsigned int* distRow = dist.data() + local * numNodes;\n                relaxRow(distRow, path.data() + local * numNodes, kRow, distRow[k], ku, numNodes);\n                std::copy_n(distRow, numNodes, buf[nxt]);\n            }\n            MPI_Ibcast(buf[nxt], intN, MPI_UNSIGNED, rowOwner(next, numNodes, size),\n                       MPI_COMM_WORLD, &req[nxt]);\n        }\n\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < localRows; ++i) {\n            if (ownsNext && firstRow + i == next) {\n                continue;  // already relaxed above\n            }\n            unsigned int* distRow = dist.data() + i * numNodes;\n            relaxRow(distRow, path.data() + i * numNodes, kRow, distRow[k], ku, numNodes);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {\n    // Basic sanity checks\n\n    // 1. Diagonal should be zero\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {\n        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\\n\", i, i);\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]\n    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++j) {\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {\n                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];\n                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];\n                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];\n\n                // Check for overflow before addition\n                if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF) {\n                    if (distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {\n                        printf(\"Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]\\n\",\n                               i, j, k);\n                        return false;\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of nodes in the graph (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n Every Floyd-Warshall step k needs the\n// full row k, which the owning rank broadcasts to everyone.\n\n// Block row distribution: first (n % size) ranks get one extra row.\ninline size_t rowBegin(const size_t numNodes, const int rank, const int size) noexcept {\n    const size_t base = numNodes / static_cast<size_t>(size);\n    const size_t rem = numNodes % static_cast<size_t>(size);\n    const size_t r = static_cast<size_t>(rank);\n    return r * base + std::min(r, rem);\n}\n\ninline int rowOwner(const size_t row, const size_t numNodes, const int size) noexcept {\n    const size_t base = numNodes / static_cast<size_t>(size);\n    const size_t rem = numNodes % static_cast<size_t>(size);\n    if (base == 0) {\n        return static_cast<int>(row);\n    }\n    if (row < rem * (base + 1)) {\n        return static_cast<int>(row / (base + 1));\n    }\n    return static_cast<int>(rem + (row - rem * (base + 1)) / base);\n}\n\n// Generates exactly the same sequence as the serial code, but keeps only the rows\n// owned by this rank (the RNG is inherently sequential, so the cheap O(n^2) stream\n// is replayed everywhere while only O(n^2 / p) values are stored).\nvoid initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes,\n                              const size_t firstRow, const size_t localRows,\n                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {\n    unsigned int seed = 42;\n    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;\n\n    const size_t begin = firstRow * numNodes;\n    const size_t end = begin + localRows * numNodes;\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < localRows; ++i) {\n        dist[i * numNodes + (firstRow + i)] = 0;\n    }\n}\n\n// The serial initialization assigns path[idx2(i, j, n)] = j for every (i, j),\n// i.e. every entry of a row equals that row's index.\nvoid initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes,\n                          const size_t firstRow, const size_t localRows) {\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < localRows; ++i) {\n        std::fill_n(path.begin() + i * numNodes, numNodes,\n                    static_cast<unsigned int>(firstRow + i));\n    }\n}\n\n// Updates one owned row against the broadcast pivot row k.\nstatic inline void relaxRow(unsigned int* __restrict distRow, unsigned int* __restrict pathRow,\n                            const unsigned int* __restrict kRow, const unsigned int distIK,\n                            const unsigned int k, const size_t numNodes) {\n    for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {\n        const unsigned int newDist = distIK + kRow[j];\n        const bool better = newDist < distRow[j];\n        // Branch-free so the compiler can vectorize both updates with a blend.\n        distRow[j] = better ? newDist : distRow[j];\n        pathRow[j] = better ? k : pathRow[j];\n    }\n}\n\nvoid floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,\n                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path,\n                   const size_t numNodes, const size_t firstRow, const size_t localRows,\n                   const int size) {\n    // Distributed Floyd-Warshall. For step k the owner of row k broadcasts it; all\n    // ranks then relax their own rows against it. The pivot row for step k+1 is\n    // produced (and broadcast non-blockingly) before the bulk of step k's work, so\n    // the broadcast overlaps with computation.\n    if (numNodes == 0) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    std::vector<unsigned int> kRowBuf(2 * numNodes);\n    unsigned int* buf[2] = {kRowBuf.data(), kRowBuf.data() + numNodes};\n    MPI_Request req[2] = {MPI_REQUEST_NULL, MPI_REQUEST_NULL};\n\n    const size_t lastRow = firstRow + localRows;\n    const int intN = static_cast<int>(numNodes);\n\n    // Prime the pipeline with row 0.\n    {\n        const int owner = rowOwner(0, numNodes, size);\n        if (0 >= firstRow && 0 < lastRow) {\n            std::copy_n(dist.data(), numNodes, buf[0]);\n        }\n        MPI_Ibcast(buf[0], intN, MPI_UNSIGNED, owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &req[0]);\n    }\n\n    for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {\n        const int cur = static_cast<int>(k & 1);\n        const int nxt = cur ^ 1;\n        MPI_Wait(&req[cur], MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);\n        const unsigned int* __restrict kRow = buf[cur];\n        const unsigned int ku = static_cast<unsigned int>(k);\n\n        // Produce and publish the next pivot row first, so its broadcast can\n        // progress while the remaining rows are relaxed.\n        const size_t next = k + 1;\n        bool ownsNext = false;\n        if (next < numNodes) {\n            ownsNext = (next >= firstRow && next < lastRow);\n            if (ownsNext) {\n                const size_t local = next - firstRow;\n                unsigned int* distRow = dist.data() + local * numNodes;\n                relaxRow(distRow, path.data() + local * numNodes, kRow, distRow[k], ku, numNodes);\n                std::copy_n(distRow, numNodes, buf[nxt]);\n            }\n            MPI_Ibcast(buf[nxt], intN, MPI_UNSIGNED, rowOwner(next, numNodes, size),\n                       MPI_COMM_WORLD, &req[nxt]);\n        }\n\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < localRows; ++i) {\n            if (ownsNext && firstRow + i == next) {\n                continue;  // already relaxed above\n            }\n            unsigned int* distRow = dist.data() + i * numNodes;\n            relaxRow(distRow, path.data() + i * numNodes, kRow, distRow[k], ku, numNodes);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {\n    // Basic sanity checks\n\n    // 1. Diagonal should be zero\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {\n        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\\n\", i, i);\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]\n    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++j) {\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {\n                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];\n                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];\n                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];\n\n                // Check for overflow before addition\n                if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF) {\n                    if (distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {\n                        printf(\"Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]\\n\",\n                               i, j, k);\n                        return false;\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of nodes in the graph (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help Every Floyd-Warshall step k needs the\n// full row k, which the owning rank broadcasts to everyone.\n\n// Block row distribution: first (n % size) ranks get one extra row.\ninline size_t rowBegin(const size_t numNodes, const int rank, const int size) noexcept {\n    const size_t base = numNodes / static_cast<size_t>(size);\n    const size_t rem = numNodes % static_cast<size_t>(size);\n    const size_t r = static_cast<size_t>(rank);\n    return r * base + std::min(r, rem);\n}\n\ninline int rowOwner(const size_t row, const size_t numNodes, const int size) noexcept {\n    const size_t base = numNodes / static_cast<size_t>(size);\n    const size_t rem = numNodes % static_cast<size_t>(size);\n    if (base == 0) {\n        return static_cast<int>(row);\n    }\n    if (row < rem * (base + 1)) {\n        return static_cast<int>(row / (base + 1));\n    }\n    return static_cast<int>(rem + (row - rem * (base + 1)) / base);\n}\n\n// Generates exactly the same sequence as the serial code, but keeps only the rows\n// owned by this rank (the RNG is inherently sequential, so the cheap O(n^2) stream\n// is replayed everywhere while only O(n^2 / p) values are stored).\nvoid initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes,\n                              const size_t firstRow, const size_t localRows,\n                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {\n    unsigned int seed = 42;\n    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;\n\n    const size_t begin = firstRow * numNodes;\n    const size_t end = begin + localRows * numNodes;\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes * numNodes; ++i) {\n        const unsigned int value = rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);\n        if (i >= begin && i < end) {\n            dist[i - begin] = value;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < localRows; ++i) {\n        dist[i * numNodes + (firstRow + i)] = 0;\n    }\n}\n\n// The serial initialization assigns path[idx2(i, j, n)] = j for every (i, j),\n// i.e. every entry of a row equals that row's index.\nvoid initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes,\n                          const size_t firstRow, const size_t localRows) {\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < localRows; ++i) {\n        std::fill_n(path.begin() + i * numNodes, numNodes,\n                    static_cast<unsigned int>(firstRow + i));\n    }\n}\n\n// Updates one owned row against the broadcast pivot row k.\nstatic inline void relaxRow(unsigned int* __restrict distRow, unsigned int* __restrict pathRow,\n                            const unsigned int* __restrict kRow, const unsigned int distIK,\n                            const unsigned int k, const size_t numNodes) {\n    for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {\n        const unsigned int newDist = distIK + kRow[j];\n        const bool better = newDist < distRow[j];\n        // Branch-free so the compiler can vectorize both updates with a blend.\n        distRow[j] = better ? newDist : distRow[j];\n        pathRow[j] = better ? k : pathRow[j];\n    }\n}\n\nvoid floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,\n                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path,\n                   const size_t numNodes, const size_t firstRow, const size_t localRows,\n                   const int size, MPI_Comm comm) {\n    // Distributed Floyd-Warshall. For step k the owner of row k broadcasts it; all\n    // ranks then relax their own rows against it. The pivot row for step k+1 is\n    // produced (and broadcast non-blockingly) before the bulk of step k's work, so\n    // the broadcast overlaps with computation.\n    if (numNodes == 0) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    std::vector<unsigned int> kRowBuf(2 * numNodes);\n    unsigned int* buf[2] = {kRowBuf.data(), kRowBuf.data() + numNodes};\n    MPI_Request req[2] = {MPI_REQUEST_NULL, MPI_REQUEST_NULL};\n\n    const size_t lastRow = firstRow + localRows;\n    const int intN = static_cast<int>(numNodes);\n\n    // Prime the pipeline with row 0.\n    {\n        const int owner = rowOwner(0, numNodes, size);\n        if (0 >= firstRow && 0 < lastRow) {\n            std::copy_n(dist.data(), numNodes, buf[0]);\n        }\n        MPI_Ibcast(buf[0], intN, MPI_UNSIGNED, owner, comm, &req[0]);\n    }\n\n    for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {\n        const int cur = static_cast<int>(k & 1);\n        const int nxt = cur ^ 1;\n        MPI_Wait(&req[cur], MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);\n        const unsigned int* __restrict kRow = buf[cur];\n        const unsigned int ku = static_cast<unsigned int>(k);\n\n        // Produce and publish the next pivot row first, so its broadcast can\n        // progress while the remaining rows are relaxed.\n        const size_t next = k + 1;\n        bool ownsNext = false;\n        if (next < numNodes) {\n            ownsNext = (next >= firstRow && next < lastRow);\n            if (ownsNext) {\n                const size_t local = next - firstRow;\n                unsigned int* distRow = dist.data() + local * numNodes;\n                relaxRow(distRow, path.data() + local * numNodes, kRow, distRow[k], ku, numNodes);\n                std::copy_n(distRow, numNodes, buf[nxt]);\n            }\n            MPI_Ibcast(buf[nxt], intN, MPI_UNSIGNED, rowOwner(next, numNodes, size),\n                       comm, &req[nxt]);\n        }\n\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < localRows; ++i) {\n            if (ownsNext && firstRow + i == next) {\n                continue;  // already relaxed above\n            }\n            unsigned int* distRow = dist.data() + i * numNodes;\n            relaxRow(distRow, path.data() + i * numNodes, kRow, distRow[k], ku, numNodes);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {\n    // Basic sanity checks\n\n    // 1. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]\n    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) before addition\n                if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF) {\n                    if (distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {\n                        printf(\"Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]\\n\",\n                               i, j, k);\n                        return false;\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n\n    int rank = 0;\n    int size = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n          }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"Number of nodes: %zu\\n\", numNodes);\n        printf(\"MPI ranks: %d\\n\", size);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n the final barrier.\n        MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n        MPI_Finalize();\n        return 0;\n    }\n\n    const size_t firstRow = rowBegin(numNodes, rank, size);\n    const size_t localRows = Every Floyd-Warshall step k needs the\n// full row k, which the owning rank broadcasts to everyone.\n\n// Block row distribution: first (n % size) ranks get one extra row.\ninline size_t rowBegin(const size_t numNodes, const int rank, const int size) noexcept {\n    const size_t base = numNodes / static_cast<size_t>(size);\n    const size_t rem = numNodes % static_cast<size_t>(size);\n    const size_t r = static_cast<size_t>(rank);\n    return r * base + std::min(r, rem);\n}\n\ninline int rowOwner(const size_t row, const size_t numNodes, const int size) noexcept {\n    const size_t base = numNodes / static_cast<size_t>(size);\n    const size_t rem = numNodes % static_cast<size_t>(size);\n    if (base == 0) {\n        return static_cast<int>(row);\n    }\n    if (row < rem * (base + 1)) {\n        return static_cast<int>(row / (base + 1));\n    }\n    return static_cast<int>(rem + (row - rem * (base + 1)) / base);\n}\n\n// Generates exactly the same sequence as the serial code, but keeps only the rows\n// owned by this rank (the RNG is inherently sequential, so the cheap O(n^2) stream\n// is replayed everywhere while only O(n^2 / p) values are stored).\nvoid initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes,\n                              const size_t firstRow, const size_t localRows,\n                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {\n    unsigned int seed = 42;\n    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;\n\n    const size_t begin = firstRow * numNodes;\n    const size_t end = begin + localRows * numNodes;\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < localRows; ++i) {\n        std::fill_n(path.begin() + i * numNodes, numNodes,\n                    static_cast<unsigned int>(firstRow + i));\n    }\n}\n\n// Updates one owned row against the broadcast pivot row k.\nstatic inline void relaxRow(unsigned int* __restrict distRow, unsigned int* __restrict pathRow,\n                            const unsigned int* __restrict kRow, const unsigned int distIK,\n                            const unsigned int k, const size_t numNodes) {\n    for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {\n        const unsigned int newDist = distIK + kRow[j];\n        const bool better = newDist < distRow[j];\n        // Branch-free so the compiler can vectorize both updates with a blend.\n        distRow[j] = better ? newDist : distRow[j];\n        pathRow[j] = better ? k : pathRow[j];\n    }\n}\n\nvoid floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,\n                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path,\n                   const size_t numNodes, const size_t firstRow, const size_t localRows,\n                   const int size, MPI_Comm comm) {\n    // Distributed Floyd-Warshall. For step k the owner of row k broadcasts it; all\n    // ranks then relax their own rows against it. The pivot row for step k+1 is\n    // produced (and broadcast non-blockingly) before the bulk of step k's work, so\n    // the broadcast overlaps with computation.\n    if (numNodes == 0) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    std::vector<unsigned int> kRowBuf(2 * numNodes);\n    unsigned int* buf[2] = {kRowBuf.data(), kRowBuf.data() + numNodes};\n    MPI_Request req[2] = {MPI_REQUEST_NULL, MPI_REQUEST_NULL};\n\n    const size_t lastRow = firstRow + localRows;\n    const int intN = static_cast<int>(numNodes);\n\n    // Prime the pipeline with row 0.\n    {\n        const int owner = rowOwner(0, numNodes, size);\n        if (0 >= firstRow && 0 < lastRow) {\n            std::copy_n(dist.data(), numNodes, buf[0]);\n        }\n        MPI_Ibcast(buf[0], intN, MPI_UNSIGNED, owner, comm, &req[0]);\n    }\n\n    for (size_t k = 0; Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"Number of nodes: %zu\\n\", numNodes);\n        printf(\"MPI ranks: %d\\n\", size);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n Every Floyd-Warshall step k needs the\n// full row k, which the owning rank broadcasts to everyone.\n\n// Block row distribution: first (n % size) ranks get one extra row.\ninline size_t rowBegin(const size_t numNodes, const int rank, const int size) noexcept {\n    const size_t base = numNodes / static_cast<size_t>(size);\n    const size_t rem = numNodes % static_cast<size_t>(size);\n    const size_t r = static_cast<size_t>(rank);\n    return r * base + std::min(r, rem);\n}\n\ninline int rowOwner(const size_t row, const size_t numNodes, const int size) noexcept {\n    const size_t base = numNodes / static_cast<size_t>(size);\n    const size_t rem = numNodes % static_cast<size_t>(size);\n    if (base == 0) {\n        return static_cast<int>(row);\n    }\n    if (row < rem * (base + 1)) {\n        return static_cast<int>(row / (base + 1));\n    }\n    return static_cast<int>(rem + (row - rem * (base + 1)) / base);\n}\n\n// Generates exactly the same sequence as the serial code, but keeps only the rows\n// owned by this rank (the RNG is inherently sequential, so the cheap O(n^2) stream\n// is replayed everywhere while only O(n^2 / p) values are stored).\nvoid initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes,\n                              const size_t firstRow, const size_t localRows,\n                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {\n    unsigned int seed = 42;\n    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;\n\n    const size_t begin = firstRow * numNodes;\n    const size_t end = begin + localRows * numNodes;\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes * numNodes; ++i) {\n        const unsigned int value = rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);\n        if (i >= begin && i < end) {\n            dist[i - begin] = value;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < localRows; ++i) {\n        dist[i * numNodes + (firstRow + i)] = 0;\n    }\n}\n\n// The serial initialization assigns path[idx2(i, j, n)] = j for every (i, j),\n// i.e. every entry of a row equals that row's index.\nvoid initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes,\n                          const size_t firstRow, const size_t localRows) {\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < localRows; ++i) {\n        std::fill_n(path.begin() + i * numNodes, numNodes,\n                    static_cast<unsigned int>(firstRow + i));\n    }\n}\n\n// Updates one owned row against the broadcast pivot row k.\nstatic inline void relaxRow(unsigned int* __restrict distRow, unsigned int* __restrict pathRow,\n                            const unsigned int* __restrict kRow, const unsigned int distIK,\n                            const unsigned int k, const size_t numNodes) {\n    for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {\n        const unsigned int newDist = distIK + kRow[j];\n        const bool better = newDist < distRow[j];\n        // Branch-free so the compiler can vectorize both updates with a blend.\n        distRow[j] = better ? newDist : distRow[j];\n        pathRow[j] = better ? k : pathRow[j];\n    }\n}\n\nvoid floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,\n                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path,\n                   const size_t numNodes, const size_t firstRow, const size_t localRows,\n                   const int size, MPI_Comm comm) {\n    // Distributed Floyd-Warshall. For step k the owner of row k broadcasts it; all\n    // ranks then relax their own rows against it. The pivot row for step k+1 is\n    // produced (and broadcast non-blockingly) before the bulk of step k's work, so\n    // the broadcast overlaps with computation.\n    if (numNodes == 0) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    std::vector<unsigned int> kRowBuf(2 * numNodes);\n    unsigned int* buf[2] = {kRowBuf.data(), kRowBuf.data() + numNodes};\n    MPI_Request req[2] = {MPI_REQUEST_NULL, MPI_REQUEST_NULL};\n\n    const size_t lastRow = firstRow + localRows;\n    const int intN = static_cast<int>(numNodes);\n\n    // Prime the pipeline with row 0.\n    {\n        const int owner = rowOwner(0, numNodes, size);\n        if (0 >= firstRow && 0 < lastRow) {\n            std::copy_n(dist.data(), numNodes, buf[0]);\n        }\n        MPI_Ibcast(buf[0], intN, MPI_UNSIGNED, owner, comm, &req[0]);\n    }\n\n    for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {\n        const int cur = static_cast<int>(k & 1);\n        const int nxt = cur ^ 1;\n        MPI_Wait(&req[cur], MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);\n        const unsigned int* __restrict kRow = buf[cur];\n        const unsigned int ku = static_cast<unsigned int>(k);\n\n        // Produce and publish the next pivot row first, so its broadcast can\n        // progress while the remaining rows are relaxed.\n        const size_t next = k + 1;\n        bool ownsNext = false;\n        if (next < numNodes) {\n            ownsNext = (next >= firstRow && next < lastRow);\n            if (ownsNext) {\n                const size_t local = next - firstRow;\n                unsigned int* distRow = dist.data() + local * numNodes;\n                relaxRow(distRow, path.data() + local * numNodes, kRow, distRow[k], ku, numNodes);\n                std::copy_n(distRow, numNodes, buf[nxt]);\n            }\n            MPI_Ibcast(buf[nxt], intN, MPI_UNSIGNED, rowOwner(next, numNodes, size),\n                       comm, &req[nxt]);\n        }\n\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < localRows; ++i) {\n            if (ownsNext && firstRow + i == next) {\n                continue;  // already relaxed above\n            }\n            unsigned int* distRow = dist.data() + i * numNodes;\n            relaxRow(distRow, path.data() + i * numNodes, kRow, distRow[k], ku, numNodes);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {\n    // Basic sanity checks\n\n    // 1. Diagonal should be zero\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {\n        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\\n\", i, i);\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]\n    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++j) {\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {\n                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];\n                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];\n                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];\n\n                // Check for overflow before addition\n                if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF) {\n                    if (distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {\n                        printf(\"Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]\\n\",\n                               i, j, k);\n                        return false;\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of nodes in the graph (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n\n    int rank = 0;\n    int size = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &size);\n\n    size_t numNodes = 512;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numNodes = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"Number of nodes: %zu\\n\", numNodes);\n        printf(\"MPI ranks: %d\\n\", size);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    }\n\n    // With more ranks than rows some ranks have no work; split them off so the\n    // collectives below run on exactly the participating ranks.\n    MPI_Comm comm = MPI_COMM_WORLD;\n    if (static_cast<size_t>(size) > numNodes) {\n        const int active = static_cast<int>(numNodes);\n        MPI_Comm_split(MPI_COMM_WORLD, rank < active ? 0 : MPI_UNDEFINED, rank, &comm);\n        if (rank >= active) {\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        }\n        size = active;\n    }\n\n    const size_t firstRow = rowBegin(numNodes, rank, size);\n    const size_t localRows = rowBegin(numNodes, rank + 1, size) - firstRow;\n\n    // Allocate local row blocks\n    std::vector<unsigned int> dist(localRows * numNodes);\n    std::vector<unsigned int> path(localRows * numNodes);\n\n    // Initialize\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Initializing graph...\\n\");\n    }\n    initializeDistanceMatrix(dist, numNodes, firstRow, localRows, 1, MAX_DISTANCE);\n    initializePathMatrix(path, numNodes, firstRow, localRows);\n\n    // Run Floyd-Warshall\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Computing shortest paths...\\n\");\n    }\n    MPI_Barrier(comm);\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    floydWarshall(dist, path, numNodes, firstRow, localRows, size, comm);\n\n    MPI_Barrier(comm);\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n\n        // Calculate operations per second\n        // Floyd-Warshall has O(n³) complexity\n        double ops = (double)numNodes * numNodes * numNodes;\n        double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;\n        printf(\"Performance: %.3f GOPS\\n\", gflops);\n    }\n\n    // Result printing and validation operate on the full matrix, so collect it on\n    // rank 0 only when actually requested.\n    if (printResults || validate) {\n        std::vector<unsigned int> full;\n        std::vector<int> counts, displs;\n        if (rank == 0) {\n {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CehkEazpaM7FXWPeBASq4","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Done. Both files in `./floydwarshall/` were modified in place; the executable is still `floydwarshall`.\n\n## Approach\n\nThe original flat index `idx2(j,i,n) == i*n + j` means the matrix is effectively **row-major with `i` as the row**, and the innermost `j` loop is contiguous. That makes a **1D row-block decomposition** the natural fit: rank *r* owns rows `[rowBegin(r), rowBegin(r+1))` (remainder rows spread over the first ranks), so each rank holds only `n²/p` elements — genuinely distributed, no replicated matrix.\n\nKey points in `floydwarshall.cpp`:\n\n- **Pivot-row broadcast** (`floydwarshall.cpp:95`): step *k* needs the whole row *k* plus the local column entry `D[i][k]`, which is already local. So one broadcast of `n` uints per step, `O(n²)` total volume against `O(n³)` work.\n- **Communication/computation overlap**: the pivot row for step *k+1* is relaxed *first* by its owner, copied out, and published with `MPI_Ibcast` into a double buffer — then the rank relaxes its remaining rows while that broadcast is in flight. Correctness holds because row *k+1* is only touched by step *k* before it's needed.\n- **Branch-free inner loop** (`relaxRow`, `floydwarshall.cpp:83`) with `__restrict`, so GCC vectorizes both the distance and the path update as blends.\n- **Distributed initialization**: `rand_r` is a sequential stream, so each rank replays the cheap `O(n²)` sequence but stores only its own rows — bit-identical input, `O(n²/p)` memory.\n- The path matrix needs no communication; its update is purely row-local.\n- `-r`/`-v` gather the matrix on rank 0 via `MPI_Gatherv` only when actually requested, so the un-instrumented benchmark path never materializes the full matrix. The validation exit code is broadcast so all ranks return it.\n- More ranks than rows is handled with `MPI_Comm_split` rather than idle ranks on a mismatched communicator.\n\n## Correctness\n\nDistance-matrix hash matches a serial reference build exactly at np = 1, 3, 7, 16, 300, 301 (`fdfe123cf18585e3` for n=300; `0998f1bc88b15e85` for the default n=512). The path matrix was checked the same way with temporary instrumentation that I then removed and rebuilt. `-v` passes; `-h`, unknown options, `n=0` and `n=1` behave as before.\n\n## Scaling (n=4096, `--map-by l3cache --bind-to core`)\n\n| ranks | 1 | 2 | 4 | 8 | 16 | 32 | 64 | 128 |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| GOPS | 2.67 | 5.22 | 11.1 | 21.7 | 38.5 | 72.5 | 112.7 | 147.8 |\n| speedup | 1× | 2.0× | 4.2× | 8.1× | 14.5× | 27× | 42× | 55× |\n\nNear-linear to 32 ranks (slightly superlinear early on, since the per-rank working set drops into cache), tapering at high counts where the per-step broadcast latency starts to dominate the shrinking per-rank row block.\n\nOne caveat on the numbers: the host is shared and was under load ~40 during measurement, and mpirun's default `--map-by core` packs low rank counts onto a single CCD — that alone made np=8 read 3.7 GOPS instead of 21.7. The table above uses spread placement; the code itself is unchanged between those runs."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":329,"cache_read_input_tokens":49162,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":329},"output_tokens":5,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"31dfa5d6-2d83-4ecf-8c95-d66f32244803","uuid":"176c2f87-281b-4523-9791-8048cd3030f7","timestamp":"2026-09-04T04:11:59.430Z","request_id":"req_011CehkEaBTR4AjKefXfZhhB"}
